Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a supervised algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 642. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 74/4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the supervised decomposition. By minimality, the element a^642 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 74-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
